**Text**

Game 2 is over: I won as Black on material at the 40-move cap, so the match score is now 1.5 to 0.5 in my favour after two games.

The game went 1.e4 e5 2.Nf3 Nc6 3.Bb5 a6 4.Ba4 Nf6 and so on into the Closed Ruy Lopez. The decisive phase came after White's 9.Nc4 Bxc4 10.dxc4 Nxe4 (winning a pawn), then the knight fork 11...Nc5 hitting the queen on d3 and the bishop on a4, and 17...Bxc4 style exchanges that left me a piece up.
